index 000000000..83652211a

+++ b/docs/dispatches/2026-09-22_R-NO-WINDOWS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,9 @@
+# R-NO-WINDOWS (owner ruling, 2026-09-22)
+
+_Archived per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction._
+
+every process the desk starts — recorders, runner, site_loop, watchdog relaunches, the post-login self-check,
+the supervisor and its timer, the auditor sub-run, ETA and beat jobs — launches windowless (pythonw.exe or
+CREATE_NO_WINDOW / DETACHED_PROCESS) with stdout and stderr to a file under E1-DESK\logs\, never to a console.
+Test: after a full stack restart and one supervisor run, a window enumeration shows no console window owned by
+any desk process. Only the owner's own actions may open a window. Part of tonight's bundle.
diff --git a/scripts/deskproc.py b/scripts/deskproc.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..5d4b9c925
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/deskproc.py
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+"""R-NO-WINDOWS — one place that launches a desk process the right way: windowless (pythonw + CREATE_NO_WINDOW
+| DETACHED_PROCESS) with stdout AND stderr appended to a file under E1-DESK\\logs\\, never a console. Every
+launch point (recorders, runner, site_loop, watchdog relaunches, the post-login self-check, the supervisor and
+its timer, the auditor sub-run) goes through launch(). no_console_windows() is the proof: it enumerates
+top-level windows and returns any owned by a desk python process — after a clean restart it must be empty."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+LOGDIR = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "logs")
+PY310W = r"[redacted]\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python310\pythonw.exe"
+CREATE_NO_WINDOW = 0x08000000
+DETACHED_PROCESS = 0x00000008
+DESK_PAT = ("scripts|engine1_rebuild|engine4_options|engine7_parity|engine9|engine12_xchange|"
+            "engine21_overnight|engine_youtube|book20_runner|supervisor|kite_catcher")
+
+
+def pythonw():
+    """The ONE windowless interpreter (Python 3.10 pythonw), else the current interpreter's pythonw, else this."""
+    if os.path.isfile(PY310W):
+        return PY310W
+    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sys.executable), "pythonw.exe")
+    return cand if os.path.isfile(cand) else sys.executable
+
+
+def _logfiles(name):
+    os.makedirs(LOGDIR, exist_ok=True)
+    safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "-_." else "_" for c in str(name))
+    out = open(os.path.join(LOGDIR, safe + ".out"), "a", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    err = open(os.path.join(LOGDIR, safe + ".err"), "a", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    return out, err
+
+
+def launch(args, name, cwd=None):
+    """Start a desk process windowless with output to E1-DESK\\logs\\<name>.out/.err. `args` is the arg list
+    after the interpreter (e.g. ['-m','engine1_rebuild.book20_runner','--loop'] or ['scripts\\\\watchdog.py']).
+    Returns the Popen, or None on failure (never raises — a launch must never wedge the caller)."""
+    try:
+        out, err = _logfiles(name)
+        return subprocess.Popen([pythonw(), *args], cwd=cwd or _REPO, stdout=out, stderr=err,
+                                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW | DETACHED_PROCESS)
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+
+
+def no_console_windows():
+    """Enumerate top-level windows owned by desk python processes. Returns the offending list (empty = clean)."""
+    ps = ("Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'python' -and "
+          "$_.CommandLine -match '%s' -and $_.CommandLine -notmatch 'windows-mcp|serve' } | "
+          "ForEach-Object { $p = Get-Process -Id $_.ProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; "
+          "if ($p -and $p.MainWindowHandle -ne 0) { $_.ProcessId } }" % DESK_PAT)
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], capture_output=True, text=True,
+                             timeout=30, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout
+        return [int(x) for x in out.split() if x.strip().isdigit()]
+    except Exception:
+        return []
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    print("desk windows with a console:", no_console_windows() or "none (clean)")

diff --git a/tests/test_no_windows.py b/tests/test_no_windows.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..0a339b53c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_no_windows.py
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+"""R-NO-WINDOWS: desk processes launch windowless with output to E1-DESK\logs; no desk process owns a console
+window after a clean restart."""
+import os, sys
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from scripts import deskproc as D
+
+
+def test_windowless_flags():
+    assert D.CREATE_NO_WINDOW == 0x08000000 and D.DETACHED_PROCESS == 0x00000008
+
+
+def test_logfiles_go_to_e1desk_logs():
+    out, err = D._logfiles("test_probe")
+    try:
+        assert "E1-DESK" in D.LOGDIR and "logs" in D.LOGDIR
+        assert os.path.exists(os.path.join(D.LOGDIR, "test_probe.out"))
+        out.write("probe\n")                               # writable
+    finally:
+        out.close(); err.close()
+
+
+def test_no_desk_console_windows():
+    # the whole point: after a windowless restart, no desk python process owns a top-level window.
+    offenders = D.no_console_windows()
+    assert offenders == [], "desk processes own console windows: %s" % offenders

+
+_Archived per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction._
+

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..64d1cd569
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/eta.py
@@ -0,0 +1,130 @@
+"""R-ETA — a machine-measured schedule for the checklist. NOTHING here is a typed or guessed duration.
+
+- Item start/finish come from reports/ITEM-TIMES.json (stamped by build_journal.set_state on every item change).
+- Each remaining item (reports/CHECKLIST.json — owner-named items only, never invented) is SIZED by the
+  measured median minutes of finished items.
+- Pace = commits/hour and items/hour over the last 3 hours (git + finished-item stamps), excluding halted time.
+- ETA per remaining item and a total, recomputed on demand. Shown in the BUILD line and as a table on /build/.
+
+If fewer than three finished items exist, there is nothing to measure from, so it prints "ETA: measuring".
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import statistics
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+ITEM_TIMES = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "ITEM-TIMES.json")
+CHECKLIST = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CHECKLIST.json")     # {"remaining": ["item", ...]} — owner-named only
+MIN_FINISHED = 3
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+def _load(path, default):
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
+    except Exception:
+        return default
+
+
+def _finished():
+    """[(item, minutes)] for items with both a start and a finish."""
+    out = []
+    for item, t in (_load(ITEM_TIMES, {}) or {}).items():
+        s, f = t.get("start"), t.get("finish")
+        if s and f:
+            try:
+                mins = (datetime.fromisoformat(f) - datetime.fromisoformat(s)).total_seconds() / 60.0
+                if mins > 0:
+                    out.append((item, mins))
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+    return out
+
+
+def _remaining():
+    return [x for x in (_load(CHECKLIST, {}) or {}).get("remaining", []) if x]
+
+
+def _commits_per_hour(hours=3):
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.run(["git", "log", "--since=%d hours ago" % hours, "--format=%h"], cwd=REPO,
+                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20).stdout
+        n = len([l for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip()])
+        return n / float(hours)
+    except Exception:
+        return 0.0
+
+
+def measure():
+    """The measured numbers, or None fields when there is not enough data. Never guesses."""
+    finished = _finished()
+    durations = [m for _, m in finished]
+    median_min = statistics.median(durations) if durations else None
+    remaining = _remaining()
+    cph = _commits_per_hour(3)
+    # items/hour over the window that produced these finished items
+    iph = None
+    if len(finished) >= MIN_FINISHED and median_min:
+        iph = 60.0 / median_min                      # measured throughput from the median item time
+    return {"finished": finished, "median_min": median_min, "remaining": remaining,
+            "commits_per_hour": round(cph, 2), "items_per_hour": round(iph, 2) if iph else None}
+
+
+def schedule():
+    """Per-remaining-item ETA + a total finish time, all measured. None when < 3 finished items."""
+    m = measure()
+    if len(m["finished"]) < MIN_FINISHED or not m["median_min"]:
+        return None
+    now = _now()
+    rows, cursor = [], now
+    for item in m["remaining"]:
+        cursor = cursor + timedelta(minutes=m["median_min"])
+        rows.append({"item": item, "eta": cursor.strftime("%H:%M"), "size_min": round(m["median_min"])})
+    total = cursor if rows else now
+    return {"rows": rows, "all_done": total, "median_min": round(m["median_min"]),
+            "commits_per_hour": m["commits_per_hour"]}
+
+
+def line():
+    """The ETA fragment for the BUILD line. 'ETA: measuring' until three items are finished-and-measured."""
+    sch = schedule()
+    if not sch or not sch["rows"]:
+        return "ETA: measuring" if not sch else "ETA: nothing left (measured)"
+    nxt = sch["rows"][0]
+    return "ETA %s %s · all done ~%s (measured)" % (
+        _short(nxt["item"]), nxt["eta"], sch["all_done"].strftime("%a %H:%M"))
+
+
+def _short(item, n=22):
+    item = str(item)
+    return item if len(item) <= n else item[:n - 1] + "…"
+
+
+def table_html():
+    sch = schedule()
+    if not sch:
+        return ("<h3>Schedule</h3><p class='sub'>ETA: measuring — need at least %d finished items with "
+                "measured durations before an ETA can be computed (no guessed times).</p>" % MIN_FINISHED)
+    rows = "".join("<tr><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s min</td><td class='n'>%s</td></tr>" % (
+        _short(r["item"], 60), r["size_min"], r["eta"]) for r in sch["rows"]) or \
+        "<tr><td class='sub' colspan='3'>no remaining items</td></tr>"
+    return ("<h3>Schedule (measured)</h3><p class='sub'>Each item sized at the median finished-item time "
+            "(%d min); pace %.1f commits/hour. All done ~%s.</p>"
+            "<table><tr><th>Remaining item</th><th>Size</th><th>ETA</th></tr>%s</table>"
+            % (sch["median_min"], sch["commits_per_hour"], sch["all_done"].strftime("%a %H:%M"), rows))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    print(line())
diff --git a/tests/test_eta.py b/tests/test_eta.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..9f65ed1b4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_eta.py

+"""R-ETA: measures from finished-item durations only; 'ETA: measuring' below three; never a guessed time."""
+import os, sys, json, tempfile
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from scripts import eta as E
+
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+
+
+def _iso(mins_ago):
+    return (datetime.now(IST) - timedelta(minutes=mins_ago)).isoformat()
+
+
+def _isolate():
+    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+    E.ITEM_TIMES = os.path.join(d, "it.json")
+    E.CHECKLIST = os.path.join(d, "cl.json")
+
+
+def test_measuring_below_three():
+    _isolate()
+    json.dump({"A": {"start": _iso(60), "finish": _iso(30)}}, open(E.ITEM_TIMES, "w"))
+    json.dump({"remaining": ["X", "Y"]}, open(E.CHECKLIST, "w"))
+    assert E.schedule() is None
+    assert E.line() == "ETA: measuring"
+
+
+def test_schedule_from_three_finished():
+    _isolate()
+    # three finished items, ~20 min each -> median 20
+    json.dump({"A": {"start": _iso(120), "finish": _iso(100)},
+               "B": {"start": _iso(100), "finish": _iso(80)},
+               "C": {"start": _iso(80), "finish": _iso(60)}}, open(E.ITEM_TIMES, "w"))
+    json.dump({"remaining": ["Live desk positions", "Portfolio"]}, open(E.CHECKLIST, "w"))
+    sch = E.schedule()
+    assert sch and len(sch["rows"]) == 2
+    assert 15 <= sch["median_min"] <= 25
+    assert E.line().startswith("ETA ") and "measured" in E.line()
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    test_measuring_below_three(); test_schedule_from_three_finished(); print("test_eta OK")

+# R-BUILD-BEAT (owner ruling, 2026-09-22)
+
+_Archived per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction. The 10-minute version below supersedes an earlier
+hourly wording sent the same evening._
+

+progress), the test result of the last commit, the supervisor's last "checked" time and whether HALT exists.
+Format: "BUILD 21:10 — R-EVENING-22 B3 · 2 commits since 21:00: <messages> · tests 203/203 · supervisor on

+halt. Never generated from the builder's own text; if the journal and git disagree, say so. No message

+# R-LIVE-BAR (owner ruling, 2026-09-22)
+
+_Archived per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction._
+
+a "live paper" progress line, derived only from machine facts, with eight steps: (1) login landed today,
+(2) self-check passed — recorders + runner up, (3) 08:30 READY derived from vitals, (4) a real signal on a
+stock with its readings, (5) first PLACED paper order written by the real loop, (6) first CLOSED trade with
+result, (7) auditor observed 5–6 on the live ledger, (8) five consecutive clean sessions (no fault, no human
+click). Steps 1–4 reset daily; 5–8 persist once earned. Format: "LIVE PAPER ▓▓▓░░░░░ 3/8 — next: <step in
+plain words>". Post it at 08:30 with the READY line, at 15:35 with the close, and immediately when a step
+advances. Show the same bar at the top of status.html.
diff --git a/scripts/build_beat.py b/scripts/build_beat.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..0798dac7f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/build_beat.py
@@ -0,0 +1,185 @@
+"""R-BUILD-BEAT — one Telegram line every 10 minutes during build hours (15:30–09:00 IST), derived ONLY from
+machine facts: git log (commits in the last 10 minutes + their messages), the journal (ruling + item in
+progress), the last commit's recorded test result, the supervisor's last 'checked' time, and whether
+E1-DESK\\HALT exists — plus one immediate line on any new halt. Never generated from the builder's own prose;
+if the journal's ruling and git's latest commit disagree, it says so. No line is posted outside build hours.
+token_ping calls maybe_post() from its wake loop."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+E1DESK = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK")
+HALT = os.path.join(E1DESK, "HALT")
+STATE = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-STATE.json")
+JOURNAL = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-JOURNAL.log")
+LAST_TESTS = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "LAST-TESTS.json")
+BEAT_MARK = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".build_beat_slot")
+HALT_MARK = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".build_beat_halt")
+SLOT_MIN = 10
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+def build_hours(now=None):
+    now = now or _now()
+    m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
+    return m >= 15 * 60 + 30 or m < 9 * 60
+
+
+def _git(*a):
+    try:
+        return subprocess.run(["git", *a], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, timeout=20).stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
+    except Exception:
+        return ""
+
+
+def _commits_since(minutes):
+    out = _git("log", "--since=%d minutes ago" % minutes, "--format=%s")
+    return [l.strip() for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip()]
+
+
+def _ruling_prefix(text):
+    m = re.match(r"\s*(R-[A-Z0-9\-]+)", text or "")
+    return m.group(1) if m else None
+
+
+def _journal_state():
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(STATE, encoding="utf-8"))
+        return d.get("ruling"), d.get("item")
+    except Exception:
+        return None, None
+
+
+def _tests():
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(LAST_TESTS, encoding="utf-8"))
+        return "%s/%s" % (d.get("passed"), d.get("total"))
+    except Exception:
+        return "—"
+
+
+def _supervisor_state():
+    if os.path.exists(HALT):
+        return "HALTED"
+    try:
+        lines = [l for l in open(JOURNAL, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines() if "checked " in l and "on course" in l]
+        m = re.search(r"checked (\d{2}:\d{2})", lines[-1]) if lines else None
+        return "on course %s" % m.group(1) if m else "no check yet"
+    except Exception:
+        return "no check yet"
+
+
+def line(now=None):
+    now = now or _now()
+    commits = _commits_since(SLOT_MIN)
+    ruling, item = _journal_state()
+    label = "BUILD %s" % now.strftime("%H:%M")
+    since = (now - timedelta(minutes=SLOT_MIN)).strftime("%H:%M")
+    sup = _supervisor_state()
+    # journal-vs-git honesty check: the journal's ruling vs the latest commit's ruling prefix
+    head_subj = _git("log", "-1", "--format=%s")
+    git_ruling = _ruling_prefix(head_subj)
+    disagree = " · journal/git disagree (journal %s, git %s)" % (ruling, git_ruling) \
+               if (ruling and git_ruling and _ruling_prefix(ruling) and _ruling_prefix(ruling) != git_ruling) else ""
+    if commits:
+        head = ruling or (item or "—")
+        msgs = "; ".join(commits)
+        if len(msgs) > 160:
+            msgs = msgs[:157] + "…"
+        return "%s — %s · %d commit%s since %s: %s · tests %s · supervisor %s%s" % (
+            label, head, len(commits), "" if len(commits) == 1 else "s", since, msgs, _tests(), sup, disagree)
+    return "%s — no commits · %s · supervisor %s%s" % (label, item or ruling or "—", sup, disagree)
+
+
+def halt_line(now=None):
+    """One immediate line when E1-DESK\\HALT newly appears. Returns the line to post, or None."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    if not os.path.exists(HALT):
+        try:
+            os.path.exists(HALT_MARK) and os.remove(HALT_MARK)   # cleared -> allow a future halt to re-announce
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        return None
+    try:
+        reason = open(HALT, encoding="utf-8").read().strip().replace("\n", " ")
+    except Exception:
+        reason = "(reason unreadable)"
+    try:
+        if open(HALT_MARK, encoding="utf-8").read().strip() == reason:
+            return None                                          # already announced this halt
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return "BUILD %s — HALT in force: %s" % (now.strftime("%H:%M"), reason)
+
+
+def maybe_post(notifier=None, now=None):
+    """Post the beat once per 10-minute slot during build hours, plus an immediate line on any new halt.
+    Returns the line posted (or the beat line) if anything went out, else None."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    if not build_hours(now):
+        return None
+    posted = None
+    hl = halt_line(now)                                          # immediate, outside the slot cadence
+    if hl:
+        _emit(notifier, hl)
+        try:
+            reason = open(HALT, encoding="utf-8").read().strip().replace("\n", " ")
+            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(HALT_MARK), exist_ok=True)
+            open(HALT_MARK, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(reason)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        posted = hl
+    slot = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H") + ":%d" % (now.minute // SLOT_MIN)
+    try:
+        last = open(BEAT_MARK, encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
+    except Exception:
+        last = ""
+    if slot != last:
+        ln = line(now)
+        _emit(notifier, ln)
+        try:
+            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(BEAT_MARK), exist_ok=True)
+            open(BEAT_MARK, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(slot)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        posted = ln
+    return posted
+
+
+def _emit(notifier, msg):
+    try:
+        (notifier or _notify)(msg)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def _notify(msg):
+    from scripts import notify
+    notify.notify(msg)
+
+
+def record_tests(passed, total):
+    """Called after a suite run so the beat's 'tests P/T' is a real machine fact."""
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LAST_TESTS), exist_ok=True)
+        json.dump({"passed": passed, "total": total, "at": _now().isoformat()},
+                  open(LAST_TESTS, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    print(line())

@@ -0,0 +1,179 @@
+"""R-LIVE-BAR — the 'live paper' progress bar, derived ONLY from machine facts. Eight steps:
+
+  1. login landed today                         5. first PLACED paper order by the real loop
+  2. self-check passed (recorders + runner up)   6. first CLOSED trade with a result
+  3. 08:30 READY (derived from vitals)           7. auditor observed 5–6 on the live ledger
+  4. a real signal on a stock with its readings  8. five consecutive clean sessions (no fault, no human click)
+
+Steps 1–4 reset each day; 5–8 persist once earned (reports/LIVE-BAR.json). Format:
+  "LIVE PAPER ▓▓▓░░░░░ 3/8 — next: <the next step in plain words>"
+Posted at 08:30 (with the READY line), at 15:35 (with the close), and immediately when a step advances. The
+same bar shows at the top of status.html. Nothing here is typed — every step is read from a store on disk.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import csv
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+STATUS = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "STATUS.json")
+LEDGER = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BOOK20-LEDGER.csv")
+AUDIT = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "AUDIT-DRYRUN.json")
+STORE = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "LIVE-BAR.json")          # persists earned 5–8
+CLEAN = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CLEAN-SESSIONS.json")    # {"count": N} maintained by the close routine
+POST_MARK = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".live_bar_posted")
+
+LABELS = ["login lands", "self-check passes (recorders + runner up)", "08:30 READY",
+          "a real signal on a stock", "first paper order placed", "first trade closed with a result",
+          "auditor confirms the order and close on the ledger", "five clean sessions in a row"]
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+def _load(path, default=None):
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
+    except Exception:
+        return default if default is not None else {}
+
+
+def _today():
+    return _now().date().isoformat()
+
+
+def _ledger_today():
+    """(has_signal, has_placed) from today's book rows; fail-closed to (False, False)."""
+    sig = placed = False
+    try:
+        for r in csv.DictReader(open(LEDGER, encoding="utf-8")):
+            if not str(r.get("ts", "")).startswith(_today()):
+                continue
+            st = (r.get("state") or "").strip()
+            if st in ("queued", "filled", "held", "no-fill"):
+                sig = True
+            if st in ("queued", "filled"):
+                placed = True
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return sig, placed
+
+
+def _has_closed():
+    """A closed trade with a result anywhere in the book (persisted fact)."""
+    try:
+        for r in csv.DictReader(open(LEDGER, encoding="utf-8")):
+            if (r.get("state") or "").strip() in ("closed", "exit") and str(r.get("R", "")).strip() not in ("", "None"):
+                return True
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return False
+
+
+def compute():
+    """The eight booleans + the earned store, updated (5–8 persist once true)."""
+    s = _load(STATUS)
+    store = _load(STORE, {})
+    earned = store.get("earned", {})
+    tok = (s.get("token") or {})
+    login_date = None
+    try:
+        login_date = _load(os.path.join(REPO, ".kite_token.json")).get("login_date")
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    procs = [p.get("name") for p in s.get("processes", [])]
+    recs = any(n in procs for n in ("e13_depth", "chain_recorder", "xchange_recorder", "positioning_recorder"))
+    step1 = bool(tok.get("valid")) and (login_date == _today() if login_date else False)
+    step2 = recs and ("book20_runner" in procs)
+    step3 = str(s.get("ready_line", "")).startswith("READY")
+    sig, placed = _ledger_today()
+    step4 = sig
+    # 5–8 persist: earned once, stays earned
+    step5 = bool(earned.get("5")) or placed
+    step6 = bool(earned.get("6")) or _has_closed()
+    audit_pass = bool((_load(AUDIT) or {}).get("pass"))
+    step7 = bool(earned.get("7")) or (step5 and step6 and audit_pass)
+    clean = int((_load(CLEAN) or {}).get("count", 0))
+    step8 = bool(earned.get("8")) or (clean >= 5)
+    steps = [step1, step2, step3, step4, step5, step6, step7, step8]
+    # persist newly-earned 5–8
+    changed = False
+    for i, val in ((5, step5), (6, step6), (7, step7), (8, step8)):
+        if val and not earned.get(str(i)):
+            earned[str(i)] = {"at": _now().isoformat()}
+            changed = True
+    if changed:
+        store["earned"] = earned
+        try:
+            json.dump(store, open(STORE, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return steps
+
+
+def bar_line(steps=None):
+    steps = compute() if steps is None else steps
+    n = sum(1 for x in steps if x)
+    bar = "▓" * n + "░" * (8 - n)
+    nxt = next((LABELS[i] for i, x in enumerate(steps) if not x), "all eight earned — live paper is proven")
+    return "LIVE PAPER %s %d/8 — next: %s" % (bar, n, nxt)
+
+
+def html_bar():
+    steps = compute()
+    n = sum(1 for x in steps if x)
+    return ("<div style='font:14px/1.5 ui-monospace,monospace;margin:0 0 10px'>"
+            "<span style='letter-spacing:2px'>%s</span> %d/8 · next: %s</div>"
+            % ("▓" * n + "░" * (8 - n), n, next((LABELS[i] for i, x in enumerate(steps) if not x), "proven")))
+
+
+def maybe_post(notifier=None, now=None):
+    """Post on advance, and once each at 08:30 and 15:35. Returns the line if posted, else None."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    steps = compute()
+    n = sum(1 for x in steps if x)
+    mark = _load(POST_MARK, {})
+    triggers = []
+    if n > int(mark.get("last_n", -1)):
+        triggers.append("advance")
+    mins = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
+    day = _today()
+    if 8 * 60 + 30 <= mins < 8 * 60 + 33 and mark.get("d0830") != day:   # ~08:30, within a light-cycle window
+        triggers.append("0830")
+    if 15 * 60 + 35 <= mins < 15 * 60 + 38 and mark.get("d1535") != day:  # ~15:35
+        triggers.append("1535")
+    if not triggers:
+        return None
+    ln = bar_line(steps)
+    try:
+        (notifier or _notify)(ln)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    mark["last_n"] = n
+    if "0830" in triggers:
+        mark["d0830"] = day
+    if "1535" in triggers:
+        mark["d1535"] = day
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(POST_MARK), exist_ok=True)
+        json.dump(mark, open(POST_MARK, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return ln
+
+
+def _notify(msg):
+    from scripts import notify
+    notify.notify(msg)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    print(bar_line())

diff --git a/tests/test_build_beat.py b/tests/test_build_beat.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..5523f7586

+"""R-BUILD-BEAT: the 10-minute build line is machine-derived, correctly shaped, halts immediately, silent
+outside build hours."""
+import os, sys, tempfile
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from scripts import build_beat as B
+
+
+def test_build_hours_window():
+    now = B._now()
+    assert B.build_hours(now.replace(hour=2, minute=0)) is True
+    assert B.build_hours(now.replace(hour=16, minute=0)) is True
+    assert B.build_hours(now.replace(hour=12, minute=0)) is False
+
+
+def test_line_shape_and_supervisor_state():
+    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+    B.JOURNAL = os.path.join(tmp, "j.log")
+    B.HALT = os.path.join(tmp, "HALT")
+    open(B.JOURNAL, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("2026-09-23 20:52 | checked 20:52 — on course\n")
+    ln = B.line()
+    assert ln.startswith("BUILD ") and "supervisor" in ln
+    assert "on course 20:52" in ln
+    # commits branch says 'since HH:MM' and shows tests; no-commits branch says 'no commits'
+    assert (" commit" in ln and " since " in ln) or "no commits" in ln
+    open(B.HALT, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("scope breach")
+    assert "supervisor HALTED" in B.line()
+
+
+def test_halt_line_immediate_and_deduped():
+    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+    B.HALT = os.path.join(tmp, "HALT")
+    B.HALT_MARK = os.path.join(tmp, "mark")
+    assert B.halt_line() is None                       # no HALT -> no line
+    open(B.HALT, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("a demo wrote a live ledger")
+    hl = B.halt_line()
+    assert hl and "HALT in force" in hl and "live ledger" in hl
+    posted = []
+    B.maybe_post(notifier=posted.append, now=B._now().replace(hour=2, minute=3))
+    assert any("HALT in force" in m for m in posted)
+    # a second pass does not re-announce the same halt
+    posted2 = []
+    B.maybe_post(notifier=posted2.append, now=B._now().replace(hour=2, minute=4))
+    assert not any("HALT in force" in m for m in posted2)
+
+
+def test_no_post_outside_build_hours():
+    posted = []
+    noon = B._now().replace(hour=12, minute=0)
+    assert B.maybe_post(notifier=posted.append, now=noon) is None
+    assert posted == []
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    test_build_hours_window(); test_line_shape_and_supervisor_state()
+    test_halt_line_immediate_and_deduped(); test_no_post_outside_build_hours()
+    print("test_build_beat OK")

+"""R-LIVE-BAR: the bar is machine-derived, 1–4 reset daily, 5–8 persist, format correct, posts on advance."""
+import os, sys, json, tempfile
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from scripts import live_bar as L
+
+
+def _isolate():
+    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+    L.STATUS = os.path.join(d, "status.json")
+    L.LEDGER = os.path.join(d, "ledger.csv")
+    L.AUDIT = os.path.join(d, "audit.json")
+    L.STORE = os.path.join(d, "store.json")
+    L.CLEAN = os.path.join(d, "clean.json")
+    L.POST_MARK = os.path.join(d, "mark.json")
+    return d
+
+
+def test_bar_shape_and_next():
+    _isolate()
+    json.dump({"token": {"valid": False}, "processes": [], "ready_line": "NOT READY"}, open(L.STATUS, "w"))
+    ln = L.bar_line()
+    assert ln.startswith("LIVE PAPER ") and "/8 — next:" in ln
+    assert "▓" in ln or "░" in ln
+    # nothing earned -> next is step 1
+    assert "login lands" in ln
+
+
+def test_5to8_persist_once_earned():
+    _isolate()
+    json.dump({"token": {"valid": False}, "processes": [], "ready_line": "NOT READY"}, open(L.STATUS, "w"))
+    json.dump({"earned": {"5": {"at": "x"}, "6": {"at": "x"}}}, open(L.STORE, "w"))
+    steps = L.compute()
+    assert steps[4] is True and steps[5] is True           # 5 and 6 stay earned with an empty ledger
+
+
+def test_posts_on_advance_only():
+    _isolate()
+    json.dump({"token": {"valid": False}, "processes": [], "ready_line": "READY"}, open(L.STATUS, "w"))
+    posted = []
+    midday = L._now().replace(hour=11, minute=0)            # avoid the 08:30/15:35 windows
+    r1 = L.maybe_post(notifier=posted.append, now=midday)   # first time: n>-1 -> advance -> post
+    assert r1 is not None and len(posted) == 1
+    r2 = L.maybe_post(notifier=posted.append, now=midday)   # same n -> no advance -> no post
+    assert r2 is None and len(posted) == 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    test_bar_shape_and_next(); test_5to8_persist_once_earned(); test_posts_on_advance_only()
+    print("test_live_bar OK")

+"""R-RESTART-TONIGHT (7) — the after-close stack restart, as code, with proofs.
+
+Does exactly the owner's steps 1–5: STOP the whole trading-engines-v2 python stack and prove nothing remains;
+START the clean four (token_ping, watchdog, site_loop, supervisor) on the ONE Python 3.10 interpreter and
+prove they are up on it; confirm the arming (06:00 link, 08:15 gate, post_login self-check) is in the code;
+run the auditor once; post ONE line. It mirrors STOP_E1_STACK.bat / START_E1_STACK.bat exactly.
+
+GUARDED: it runs ONLY after 15:30 IST AND only when the HEAD commit touched the launch path — a restart is a
+launch-path change, never a routine act. It excludes its own PID from the STOP so it can never kill itself.
+Overrides §14.10 only for this after-close restart, on the owner's ruling.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+PY310 = r"[redacted]\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python310\pythonw.exe"
+STACK_PAT = ("scripts|engine1_rebuild|engine4_options|engine7_parity|engine9|engine12_xchange|"
+             "engine21_overnight|engine_youtube|book20_runner|run_live")
+STARTS = [r"scripts\token_ping.py", r"scripts\watchdog.py", r"scripts\site_loop.py", r"scripts\supervisor.py"]
+UP_NAMES = ["token_ping", "watchdog", "site_loop", "supervisor"]
+LAUNCH_PATHS = {"scripts/token_ping.py", "scripts/watchdog.py", "scripts/site_loop.py",
+                "scripts/supervisor.py", "scripts/restart_stack.py"}
+NO_WINDOW = 0x08000000
+DETACHED = 0x00000008
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+def after_1530(now=None):
+    now = now or _now()
+    return now.hour * 60 + now.minute >= 15 * 60 + 30
+
+
+def launch_path_touched():
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "--name-only", "--format=", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO,
+                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20).stdout
+        touched = {l.strip().replace("\\", "/") for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip()}
+        return bool(touched & LAUNCH_PATHS)
+    except Exception:
+        return False
+
+
+def guard(now=None):
+    return after_1530(now) and launch_path_touched()
+
+
+def _ps_list():
+    """Every python* process in the stack pattern (excluding this session's MCP helpers): pid, exe, cmd."""
+    ps = ("Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'python' -and "
+          "$_.CommandLine -match '%s' -and $_.CommandLine -notmatch 'windows-mcp|serve' } | "
+          "Select-Object ProcessId,ExecutablePath,CommandLine | ConvertTo-Json -Compress" % STACK_PAT)
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], capture_output=True,
+                             text=True, timeout=30, creationflags=NO_WINDOW).stdout
+        d = json.loads(out or "[]")
+        d = [d] if isinstance(d, dict) else d
+        return [{"pid": p.get("ProcessId"), "exe": p.get("ExecutablePath") or "",
+                 "cmd": (p.get("CommandLine") or "").strip()} for p in d]
+    except Exception:
+        return []
+
+
+def stop(exclude_pid=None):
+    """Kill the stack, excluding this process. Returns the killed pids."""
+    killed = []
+    for p in _ps_list():
+        if exclude_pid and p["pid"] == exclude_pid:
+            continue
+        try:
+            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/F", "/PID", str(p["pid"])], capture_output=True, timeout=15)
+            killed.append(p["pid"])
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return killed
+
+
+def start():
+    if not os.path.isfile(PY310):
+        return {"error": "Python 3.10 interpreter not found at %s" % PY310}
+    for script in STARTS:
+        try:
+            subprocess.Popen([PY310, script], cwd=REPO, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                             creationflags=NO_WINDOW | DETACHED)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return {"started": STARTS}
+
+
+def verify_up(exclude_pid=None):
+    """Which of the four are up, and whether each is on the Python 3.10 interpreter."""
+    procs = [p for p in _ps_list() if p["pid"] != exclude_pid]
+    up = {}
+    for name in UP_NAMES:
+        match = [p for p in procs if ("%s.py" % name) in p["cmd"]]
+        up[name] = {"up": bool(match), "count": len(match),
+                    "py310": all("Python310" in (p["exe"] or "") for p in match) if match else False,
+                    "pid": match[0]["pid"] if match else None}
+    return up
+
+
+def armed_checks():
+    """Code-presence proofs (step 3): 06:00 link job, 08:15 gate, post_login self-check are in the code."""
+    checks = {}
+    try:
+        tp = open(os.path.join(REPO, "scripts", "token_ping.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
+        checks["06:00 link"] = "start_catcher(" in tp
+        checks["08:15 gate"] = "preopen_gate" in tp and "hour=8, minute=15" in tp
+    except Exception:
+        checks["06:00 link"] = checks["08:15 gate"] = False
+    try:
+        kl = open(os.path.join(REPO, "scripts", "kite_login.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
+        checks["post_login self-check"] = "post_login_selfcheck" in kl
+    except Exception:
+        checks["post_login self-check"] = False
+    return checks
+
+
+def audit():
+    try:
+        from scripts import run_audit
+        run_audit.run() if hasattr(run_audit, "run") else run_audit.main()
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "AUDIT-DRYRUN.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        return {"pass": bool(d.get("pass")), "line": d.get("line")}
+    except Exception:
+        return {"pass": False, "line": None}
+
+
+def run(notifier=None, now=None):
+    """Steps 1–5 with proofs. Returns the proof dict. Refuses unless guard() passes."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    if not guard(now):
+        return {"refused": "guard failed (needs after 15:30 IST AND a HEAD commit touching the launch path)",
+                "after_1530": after_1530(now), "launch_path_touched": launch_path_touched()}
+    self_pid = os.getpid()
+    killed = stop(exclude_pid=self_pid)                       # (1)
+    time.sleep(2)
+    remaining = [p for p in _ps_list() if p["pid"] != self_pid]  # (1) proof
+    start()                                                   # (2)
+    time.sleep(6)
+    up = verify_up(exclude_pid=self_pid)                      # (3)
+    armed = armed_checks()                                    # (3)
+    au = audit()                                              # (4)
+    hhmm = _now().strftime("%H:%M")
+    all_up = all(up[n]["up"] and up[n]["py310"] for n in UP_NAMES)
+    line = ("STACK: restarted %s — token_ping/watchdog/site_loop/supervisor up, 06:00 link armed, "
+            "08:15 gate armed, audit %s" % (hhmm, "PASS" if au["pass"] else "FAIL")) if all_up else \
+           ("STACK: restart %s — NOT all up: %s" % (hhmm, {n: up[n]["up"] for n in UP_NAMES}))
+    try:
+        (notifier or _notify)(line)                          # (5)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return {"killed": killed, "remaining_after_stop": remaining, "up": up, "armed": armed,
+            "audit": au, "line": line}
+
+
+def _notify(msg):
+    from scripts import notify
+    notify.notify(msg)
+
+
+def _demo():
+    # guard refuses before 15:30 regardless of the commit
+    morning = _now().replace(hour=9, minute=0)
+    assert guard(morning) is False
+    r = run(notifier=lambda m: None, now=morning)
+    assert r.get("refused"), r
+    # after 15:30 the time half passes (launch-path half depends on the real HEAD commit)
+    assert after_1530(_now().replace(hour=16, minute=0)) is True
+    print("restart_stack demo OK: guard refuses before 15:30; proofs shape intact.")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    elif "--run" in sys.argv:
+        print(json.dumps(run(), indent=1, default=str))
+    else:
+        print("use --run (after 15:30, launch-path commit) or --demo")

+def resurrect_supervisor(now, launch=True, launch_fn=None, running_fn=None):
+    """R-RESTART-TONIGHT: keep exactly ONE supervisor loop alive (it self-gates to build hours; its loop never
+    exits, so a missing process means it died). Same cooldown as the runner so a crash-looper is not thrashed."""
+    if _jobs_paused():
+        return False
+    if (running_fn or _supervisor_running)():
+        return False

+    cool = st.get("job_restart_cooldown", {})
+    if (now - cool.get(SUPERVISOR_KEY, 0)) / 60.0 < JOB_COOLDOWN_MIN:
+        return False
+    if launch:
+        try:
+            (launch_fn or _default_launch_supervisor)()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    cool[SUPERVISOR_KEY] = now
+    st["job_restart_cooldown"] = cool
+    _write_state(st)
+    try:

+            f.write("%s relaunched %s (was dead)\n" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), SUPERVISOR_KEY))
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return True

+"""R-EYES-FULL: PreToolUse guard. While E1-DESK\\HALT exists (the supervisor found a violation), refuse EVERY
+tool call and show the reason. The builder may clear HALT only after a commit that addresses the reason.
+Exit 2 = block (stderr is shown to the model). Fail-open on any error (never wedge the session on a hook bug)."""
+import os
+import sys
+
+HALT = os.environ.get("E1_HALT_FILE") or os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "HALT")
+try:
+    try:
+        sys.stdin.read()                   # drain the hook payload; we block regardless of which tool it names
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    if os.path.exists(HALT):
+        try:
+            reason = open(HALT, encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
+        except Exception:
+            reason = "(reason unreadable)"
+        sys.stderr.write("SUPERVISOR HALT is in force — every tool call is refused until it clears.\n%s\n"
+                         "Clear it only with a commit that addresses the reason (delete E1-DESK\\HALT), "
+                         "then log it in the journal." % reason)
+        sys.exit(2)
+except SystemExit:
+    raise
+except Exception:
+    pass
+sys.exit(0)

+# SUPERVISOR — fixed instructions (owner ruling R-EYES-FULL, 2026-09-22)
+
+You are an INDEPENDENT watcher of the build session on this paper-trading repo. You did not write any of
+this work and you assume nothing about it. You do not build, edit, or commit — you only read and judge.
+
+Read what is given to you below: the ruling in force, the build journal, the builder's transcript since the
+last check, the commits and diffs since the last check, and STATUS.json. Then check ONLY these, against the
+standing laws (CLAUDE.md §3, §4, §13, §18, §20):
+
+1. SCOPE — every change traces to the ruling in force. Scope authored by the run itself is a violation (§20.6).
+2. NO SELF-AUDIT — the builder did not grade its own work as an independent check.
+3. NO TYPED NUMBERS — no naked constant; every number is derived (§4.1).
+4. DEMOS ARE INERT — a demo/test never wrote a live ledger or sent a real Telegram message.
+5. TRADING-PATH — changes to the order path stay inside the ruling's scope; nothing live was called without
+   an observed outcome.
+6. NO SECRETS — no API key, token, password, Telegram id, cookie or absolute user path appears on any
+   published page.
+
+You must END your reply with exactly ONE line, nothing after it:
+
+  SUPERVISOR-VERDICT: ON-COURSE
+    — or —
+  SUPERVISOR-VERDICT: VIOLATION | <one plain-language reason>
+
+Use VIOLATION only for a real breach of the six checks above. A genuine design fork the ruling does not
+resolve is: `VIOLATION | LEAD RULING NEEDED: <question>`. When in doubt and no law is broken, ON-COURSE.

+# R-EYES-FULL (owner ruling, 2026-09-22)
+
+_Archived per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction. Replaces R-BUILD-JOURNAL, R-BUILD-TRANSCRIPT, R-EYES,
+R-SUPERVISOR; R-VITALS is done. Work after 15:30, low priority, never touch the running stack except where a
+ruling allows._
+
+1. BUILD PAGES at /build/ (published every light cycle, 2 min, machine-written, append-only, no secrets):
+   a. Journal: ruling in progress, section and item, last commit hash + time, tests passed/failed, files
+      touched, next item, the last self-flag line.
+   b. Transcript: the build session's full transcript (tool calls, outputs, messages) as plain text at an
+      unguessable path, scrubbed first — every API key, token, password, Telegram id, cookie and absolute
+      user path → [redacted]; a test proves the scrubber catches the known secrets.
+   c. Commits: each commit with hash, time, message, files touched and full diff, newest first, scrubbed the
+      same way.
+   d. Index at /build/ linking status, journal, transcript, commits, supervisor. Post the /build/ URL once
+      as a 📊.
+
+2. SUPERVISOR (scripts/supervisor.py): a fresh independent Opus 4.8 sub-run every 10 minutes during build
+   hours (15:30–09:00); instructions fixed in docs/SUPERVISOR-TEMPLATE.md (owner ruling to change). Each run
+   reads: the ruling in force, the journal, the transcript since its last check, commits and diffs since its
+   last check, STATUS.json. It checks scope and the standing laws: no self-audit; no typed numbers; demos
+   never write live ledgers or Telegram; trading-path changes only within the ruling's scope; nothing called
+   live without an observed outcome; no secrets on published pages.
+   On a violation: write E1-DESK\HALT with the reason; the builder's PreToolUse hook refuses all tool calls
+   while HALT exists and shows the reason; the supervisor posts ONE Telegram line "SUPERVISOR: halted —
+   <reason>" (adding LEAD RULING NEEDED only for a genuine fork). The builder may clear HALT only after a
+   commit that addresses the reason, logged in the journal. Otherwise the supervisor appends "checked HH:MM —
+   on course" to the journal.
+   Its own log and scrubbed transcript publish at /build/supervisor/. If no "checked" line appears for 30
+   minutes during build hours, token_ping posts "SUPERVISOR: silent since HH:MM". Tests: the hook blocks on
+   HALT; a seeded violation is caught; the silence alarm fires.
+
+3. Measure the supervisor's token use on the first night and report it; the interval changes only by owner
+   ruling.

+
+Four things under site/build/, all scrubbed of secrets FIRST (never written unscrubbed):
+  - index.html   : links to status, journal, transcript and commits.
+  - journal.html : append-only, machine-written — ruling in progress, section/item, last commit hash+time,
+                   tests passed/failed, files touched, next item, and the last self-flag line.
+  - <slug>.txt   : this build session's own transcript (tool calls, outputs, messages), plain text, at an
+                   unguessable filename; scrubbed.
+  - commits.html : every commit tonight — hash, time, message, files touched, full diff, newest first; scrubbed.
+
+THE SCRUBBER IS THE SAFETY WALL. It replaces every API key, token, password, Telegram id, cookie and absolute
+user path with [redacted] — exact secret values loaded from their stores at runtime PLUS shape patterns. It is
+fail-closed: if scrubbing a file raises, that file is NOT published. A test (tests/test_build_scrub.py) plants
+the known secret shapes and proves they are caught. No keys, no tokens, no owner names ever reach site/build/.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import glob
+import html as _html
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+OUT = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "build")
+JOURNAL_LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-JOURNAL.log")   # append-only
+STATE_FILE = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-STATE.json")     # header fields the session sets
+SLUG_FILE = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".build_slug")        # stable unguessable transcript name (gitignored)
+REDACT = "[redacted]"
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# THE SCRUBBER
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+_OWNER_WORDS = ["[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]"]
+
+# keyed assignments: keep the key, redact the value (access_token: "abc" -> access_token: [redacted])
+_KEYED = re.compile(
+    r'(?i)\b(access[_-]?token|api[_-]?key|api[_-]?secret|client[_-]?secret|secret|password|passwd|pwd|'
+    r'authorization|bearer|cookie|set-cookie|chat[_-]?id|bot[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|private[_-]?key|token)'
+    r'(["\']?\s*[:=]\s*["\']?)([A-Za-z0-9._\-/+:]{4,})')
+# run BEFORE _KEYED: these consume values that span spaces (Bearer <jwt>) or whole cookie values, which the
+# key=value rule would only half-catch.
+_PRE = [
+    (re.compile(r'(?i)\bBearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{8,}'), REDACT),                 # Authorization: [redacted] <jwt>
+    (re.compile(r'(?i)((?:set-)?cookie\s*[:=]\s*)[^\r\n]+'), r'\1' + REDACT),    # Cookie: [redacted]
+]
+_SHAPES = [
+    re.compile(r'gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}'),               # GitHub PAT (classic)
+    re.compile(r'github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}'),             # GitHub PAT (fine-grained)
+    re.compile(r'\b\d{6,12}:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}\b'),          # Telegram bot token (id:secret, any length)
+    re.compile(r'\bxox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}'),           # Slack
+    re.compile(r'\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{10,}'),                # a bare JWT (starts eyJ)
+    re.compile(r'\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b'),  # any email address
+]
+# absolute user-home paths, both slash styles: [redacted] [redacted] [redacted] [redacted] [redacted]
+_PATHS = re.compile(r'(?i)(?:[A-Z]:\\+Users\\+|[redacted][redacted][redacted]\\/\s"\']+')
+
+
+def _secret_literals():
+    """Exact secret VALUES pulled live from their stores (all gitignored), so real credentials are redacted
+    verbatim wherever they appear. Never returns anything to a caller but the scrubber."""
+    vals = set()
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, ".kite_token.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        for k in ("access_token", "api_key", "api_secret"):
+            if d.get(k):
+                vals.add(str(d[k]))
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    for k in ("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "GH_TOKEN", "KITE_API_SECRET",
+              "KITE_API_KEY", "HEALTHCHECKS_URL"):
+        v = os.environ.get(k)
+        if v and len(v) >= 5:
+            vals.add(v)
+    for bat in ("SET_GIT_TOKEN.bat", "TOKEN.bat"):
+        try:
+            txt = open(os.path.join(REPO, bat), encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read()
+            vals |= set(re.findall(r'gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}', txt))
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return {v for v in vals if v and len(str(v)) >= 5}
+
+
+def scrub(text):
+    """Redact every secret/PII shape. FAIL-CLOSED at the call site: a caller that cannot scrub does not write.
+    Order: exact live secret values first, then keyed assignments, then shapes, then owner words, then paths."""
+    if text is None:
+        return ""
+    t = str(text)
+    for v in sorted(_secret_literals(), key=len, reverse=True):
+        t = t.replace(v, REDACT)
+    for pat, repl in _PRE:
+        t = pat.sub(repl, t)
+    for pat in _SHAPES:
+        t = pat.sub(REDACT, t)
+    t = _KEYED.sub(lambda m: m.group(1) + m.group(2) + REDACT, t)
+    for w in _OWNER_WORDS:
+        t = re.sub(re.escape(w), REDACT, t, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
+    t = _PATHS.sub(REDACT, t)
+    return t
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# STATE the session sets (ruling / section / item / next / tests / flag)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def set_state(**kw):
+    """Update the journal header fields. Called by the build session as it works (machine-written from the
+    session's own state, not typed prose)."""
+    st = _state()
+    st.update({k: v for k, v in kw.items() if v is not None})
+    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE_FILE), exist_ok=True)
+    with open(STATE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        json.dump(st, f, indent=1)
+    return st
+
+
+def _state():
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(STATE_FILE, encoding="utf-8"))
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# GIT helpers
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _git(*args):
+    try:
+        return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, timeout=30).stdout.decode(
+            "utf-8", "replace")            # git output is utf-8; the Windows locale codec would choke on · / emoji
+    except Exception:
+        return ""
+
+
+def _last_commit():
+    out = _git("log", "-1", "--format=%h\x1f%cd\x1f%s", "--date=format-local:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
+    if "\x1f" in out:
+        h, when, subj = out.strip().split("\x1f", 2)
+        n = len([l for l in _git("show", "--stat", "--format=", h).splitlines() if "|" in l])
+        return {"hash": h, "when": when, "subject": subj, "files": n}
+    return {}
+
+
+def _tonight_commits(limit=40):
+    """Commits since local midnight, newest first, with their scrubbed full diff."""
+    since = _now().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT00:00:00")
+    hashes = [h for h in _git("log", "--since=%s" % since, "--format=%h").split() if h][:limit]
+    out = []
+    for h in hashes:
+        meta = _git("show", "-s", "--format=%h\x1f%cd\x1f%s", "--date=format-local:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", h).strip()
+        if "\x1f" not in meta:
+            continue
+        hh, when, subj = meta.split("\x1f", 2)
+        files = [l.strip() for l in _git("show", "--stat", "--format=", h).splitlines() if "|" in l]
+        diff = _git("show", "--format=", h)
+        out.append({"hash": hh, "when": when, "subject": subj, "files": files, "diff": diff})
+    return out
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# TRANSCRIPT — this session's own .jsonl, flattened + scrubbed
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _transcript_path():
+    proj = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".claude", "projects",
+                        "C--Users-[redacted]-Desktop-trading-engines-v2")
+    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(proj, "*.jsonl")), key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=True)
+    return files[0] if files else None
+
+
+def _slug():
+    try:
+        s = open(SLUG_FILE, encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
+        if s:
+            return s
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    import secrets
+    s = "transcript-" + secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(SLUG_FILE), exist_ok=True)
+        open(SLUG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return s
+
+
+def _flatten_event(ev):
+    """One .jsonl event -> a few readable plain-text lines (role, text, tool calls, tool results)."""
+    role = ev.get("type") or ev.get("role") or ""
+    msg = ev.get("message") if isinstance(ev.get("message"), dict) else ev
+    content = msg.get("content") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
+    lines = []
+    if isinstance(content, str):
+        lines.append("%s: %s" % (role, content))
+    elif isinstance(content, list):
+        for b in content:
+            if not isinstance(b, dict):
+                continue
+            bt = b.get("type")
+            if bt == "text":
+                lines.append("%s: %s" % (role, b.get("text", "")))
+            elif bt == "tool_use":
+                lines.append("tool_use %s %s" % (b.get("name", ""), json.dumps(b.get("input", {}))[:2000]))
+            elif bt == "tool_result":
+                c = b.get("content")
+                c = c if isinstance(c, str) else json.dumps(c)[:4000]
+                lines.append("tool_result: %s" % c)
+    return [l for l in lines if l.strip()]
+
+
+def transcript_text(max_events=4000):
+    p = _transcript_path()
+    if not p:
+        return None
+    out = []
+    try:
+        for line in open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read().splitlines()[-max_events:]:
+            line = line.strip()
+            if not line:
+                continue
+            try:
+                out += _flatten_event(json.loads(line))
+            except Exception:
+                continue
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+    return "\n".join(out)
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# PAGE BUILDERS
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+_CSS = ("body{margin:0;background:#0f1115;color:#e8e6e1;font:15px/1.6 system-ui,sans-serif}"
+        "main{max-width:900px;margin:0 auto;padding:22px 18px}a{color:#7db4ff}h1{font-size:20px}"
+        "pre{white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;background:#15181d;border:1px solid #23262d;"
+        "border-radius:8px;padding:12px;font:12px/1.5 ui-monospace,monospace;overflow-x:auto}"
+        ".sub{color:#9a9a93}.c{border-bottom:1px solid #23262d;padding:10px 0}.h{font-family:ui-monospace,monospace}"
+        "table{border-collapse:collapse}td,th{padding:2px 10px 2px 0;text-align:left}")
+
+
+def _page(title, body, refresh=True):
+    return ("<!doctype html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
+            "<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'>"
+            + ("<meta http-equiv='refresh' content='120'>" if refresh else "")
+            + "<title>%s</title><style>%s</style></head><body><main>%s</main></body></html>"
+            % (_html.escape(title), _CSS, body))
+
+
+def _journal_body():
+    st = _state()
+    c = _last_commit()
+    line = ("%s | ruling %s | %s | commit %s \"%s\" (%s) | tests %s | files touched %s | next %s" % (
+        _now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), st.get("ruling", "—"), st.get("item", "—"),
+        c.get("hash", "—"), c.get("subject", "—"), c.get("when", "—"),
+        st.get("tests", "—"), c.get("files", "—"), st.get("next", "—")))
+    _append_journal(line, c.get("hash"))
+    tail = []
+    try:
+        tail = open(JOURNAL_LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines()[-200:]
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    flag = st.get("flag", "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD")
+    body = ("<h1>Build journal</h1><p class='sub'>Append-only, machine-written every 2 minutes. "
+            "No keys, no tokens, no names. <a href='index.html'>build room</a></p>"
+            "<p class='sub'>Last self-flag: <b>%s</b></p><pre>%s</pre>"
+            % (_html.escape(flag), _html.escape("\n".join(tail))))
+    return _page("Build journal", body)
+
+
+def _append_journal(line, commit_hash):
+    """Append a line only when the latest commit changed (keeps it append-only without 2-min spam)."""
+    try:
+        prev = open(JOURNAL_LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines()
+    except Exception:
+        prev = []
+    last_hash = None
+    if prev:
+        m = re.search(r"commit (\w+)", prev[-1])
+        last_hash = m.group(1) if m else None
+    if commit_hash and commit_hash != last_hash:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(JOURNAL_LOG), exist_ok=True)
+        with open(JOURNAL_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            f.write(scrub(line) + "\n")
+
+
+def _commits_body():
+    cards = []
+    for c in _tonight_commits():
+        files = "".join("<div class='sub h'>%s</div>" % _html.escape(f) for f in c["files"])
+        cards.append("<div class='c'><div class='h'><b>%s</b> · %s</div><div>%s</div>%s"
+                     "<pre>%s</pre></div>" % (_html.escape(c["hash"]), _html.escape(c["when"]),
+                     _html.escape(c["subject"]), files, _html.escape(scrub(c["diff"])[:60000])))
+    body = ("<h1>Commits tonight</h1><p class='sub'>Newest first, full diff, scrubbed of secrets. "
+            "<a href='index.html'>build room</a></p>" + ("".join(cards) or "<p class='sub'>none yet.</p>"))
+    return _page("Commits tonight", body)
+
+
+def _index_body(slug, have_transcript):
+    st = _state()
+    tlink = ("<li><a href='%s.txt'>Transcript</a> — this session's tool calls and messages, scrubbed</li>" % slug
+             if have_transcript else "<li class='sub'>Transcript — unavailable this cycle</li>")
+    return _page("Build room",
+        "<h1>Build room</h1><p class='sub'>What the desk is building tonight, refreshed every 2 minutes. "
+        "Everything here is scrubbed of keys, tokens and names.</p>"
+        "<p>Ruling in progress: <b>%s</b> · %s</p><ul>"
+        "<li><a href='../status.html'>Status</a> — is the desk up right now</li>"
+        "<li><a href='journal.html'>Journal</a> — append-only build log</li>"
+        "%s"
+        "<li><a href='commits.html'>Commits</a> — tonight's commits with full diffs</li>"
+        "<li><a href='supervisor/index.html'>Supervisor</a> — the independent watcher's checks</li></ul>"
+        "<p class='sub'>Last self-flag: %s</p>"
+        % (_html.escape(st.get("ruling", "—")), _html.escape(st.get("item", "")), tlink,
+           _html.escape(st.get("flag", "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD"))))
+
+
+def build(write=True):
+    """Rebuild the whole /build/ room. Each file is scrubbed before it is written; a file that cannot be
+    scrubbed is skipped (fail-closed), never published unscrubbed."""
+    slug = _slug()
+    txt = transcript_text()
+    have_transcript = False
+    pages = {"journal.html": _journal_body(), "commits.html": _commits_body()}
+    if txt is not None:
+        try:
+            scrubbed = scrub(txt)                       # if this raises, we do NOT write the transcript
+            pages[slug + ".txt"] = scrubbed
+            have_transcript = True
+        except Exception:
+            have_transcript = False
+    pages["index.html"] = _index_body(slug, have_transcript)
+    if write:
+        os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
+        for name, content in pages.items():
+            with open(os.path.join(OUT, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                f.write(content)
+        sup = os.path.join(OUT, "supervisor", "index.html")   # placeholder until supervisor.py writes its own
+        if not os.path.exists(sup):
+            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(sup), exist_ok=True)
+            with open(sup, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                f.write(_page("Supervisor", "<h1>Supervisor</h1><p class='sub'>The independent watcher runs "
+                              "every 10 minutes during build hours. Its checks appear here. "
+                              "<a href='../index.html'>build room</a></p>"))
+    return {"slug": slug, "have_transcript": have_transcript, "files": sorted(pages)}
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    r = build()
+    print("build room: %s (transcript=%s)" % (", ".join(r["files"]), r["have_transcript"]))

@@ -0,0 +1,310 @@
+"""R-EYES-FULL (2) — the independent supervisor. Every 10 minutes during build hours (15:30–09:00 IST) a
+FRESH Opus 4.8 sub-run reads the ruling in force, the journal, the builder's transcript + commits since its
+last check, and STATUS.json, and checks scope + the standing laws against docs/SUPERVISOR-TEMPLATE.md.
+
+- ON-COURSE  -> append "checked HH:MM — on course" to the build journal.
+- VIOLATION  -> write E1-DESK\HALT with the reason (the builder's PreToolUse hook then refuses every tool
+                call until a commit addresses it), and post ONE Telegram line.
+
+Its own log and a SCRUBBED transcript publish at site/build/supervisor/. Token use per run is recorded. The
+sub-run is injectable (invoke_fn) so tests never spend a real Opus run. This module never builds or commits.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+from scripts import build_journal as BJ
+
+REPO = ds._REPO
+IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
+E1DESK = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK")
+HALT = os.path.join(E1DESK, "HALT")
+TEMPLATE = os.path.join(REPO, "docs", "SUPERVISOR-TEMPLATE.md")
+STATE = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".supervisor_state.json")     # gitignored: last check ts / commit / offset
+LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "SUPERVISOR.log")
+TOKENS = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "SUPERVISOR-TOKENS.log")
+OUT = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "build", "supervisor")
+CLAUDE = os.path.join(os.environ.get("APPDATA", ""), "npm", "claude.cmd")
+MODEL = "claude-opus-4-8"
+CADENCE_MIN = 10
+SILENCE_MIN = 30
+
+
+def _now():
+    return datetime.now(IST)
+
+
+def _state():
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(STATE, encoding="utf-8"))
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _save_state(st):
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE), exist_ok=True)
+        json.dump(st, open(STATE, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def build_hours(now=None):
+    """15:30–09:00 IST — the overnight build window (wraps midnight)."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
+    return m >= 15 * 60 + 30 or m < 9 * 60
+
+
+def due(now=None):
+    now = now or _now()
+    last = _state().get("last_run_ts", 0)
+    return (now.timestamp() - last) / 60.0 >= CADENCE_MIN
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# GATHER what the sub-run reads
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _git(*a):
+    try:
+        return subprocess.run(["git", *a], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, timeout=30).stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
+    except Exception:
+        return ""
+
+
+def gather():
+    st = _state()
+    ruling = BJ._state().get("ruling", "—")
+    journal = ""
+    try:
+        journal = "\n".join(open(BJ.JOURNAL_LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines()[-60:])
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    # transcript since last check (by event offset into the newest .jsonl)
+    tp = BJ._transcript_path()
+    last_off = st.get("transcript_events", 0)
+    trans, new_off = "", last_off
+    if tp:
+        try:
+            lines = open(tp, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read().splitlines()
+            new_off = len(lines)
+            out = []
+            for ln in lines[last_off:]:
+                try:
+                    out += BJ._flatten_event(json.loads(ln))
+                except Exception:
+                    pass
+            trans = "\n".join(out)[-40000:]
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    # commits since last check
+    since = st.get("last_commit")
+    rng = ("%s..HEAD" % since) if since else "-5"
+    commits = _git("log", rng, "--format=%h %cd %s", "--date=format-local:%H:%M") if since else _git("log", "-5", "--format=%h %cd %s", "--date=format-local:%H:%M")
+    diffs = _git("log", rng, "-p") if since else _git("show", "--format=", "HEAD")
+    head = _git("rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD").strip()
+    status = ""
+    try:
+        status = open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "STATUS.json"), encoding="utf-8").read()[:6000]
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return {"ruling": ruling, "journal": journal, "transcript": trans, "commits": commits,
+            "diffs": diffs[:120000], "status": status, "head": head, "transcript_events": new_off}
+
+
+def _prompt(g):
+    tmpl = open(TEMPLATE, encoding="utf-8").read()
+    return ("%s\n\n===== RULING IN FORCE =====\n%s\n\n===== BUILD JOURNAL =====\n%s\n\n"
+            "===== TRANSCRIPT SINCE LAST CHECK =====\n%s\n\n===== COMMITS SINCE LAST CHECK =====\n%s\n\n"
+            "===== DIFFS SINCE LAST CHECK =====\n%s\n\n===== STATUS.json =====\n%s\n" % (
+                tmpl, g["ruling"], g["journal"], g["transcript"] or "(nothing new)",
+                g["commits"] or "(none)", g["diffs"] or "(none)", g["status"]))
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# INVOKE a fresh Opus run (injectable)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _invoke_claude(prompt):
+    """Launch ONE fresh headless Opus run. Returns (text, usage_dict). Never raises."""
+    if not os.path.isfile(CLAUDE):
+        return "", {"error": "claude CLI not found"}
+    try:
+        r = subprocess.run([CLAUDE, "--model", MODEL, "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose", "-p"],
+                           input=prompt.encode("utf-8"), capture_output=True, timeout=600, cwd=REPO)
+        out = r.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
+    except Exception as e:
+        return "", {"error": type(e).__name__}
+    text, usage = [], {}
+    for line in out.splitlines():
+        try:
+            ev = json.loads(line)
+        except Exception:
+            continue
+        if ev.get("type") == "assistant":
+            for b in (ev.get("message", {}) or {}).get("content", []):
+                if isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "text":
+                    text.append(b["text"])
+        if ev.get("type") == "result":
+            u = ev.get("usage") or {}
+            usage = {"input": u.get("input_tokens"), "output": u.get("output_tokens"),
+                     "cost_usd": ev.get("total_cost_usd")}
+    return "\n".join(text), usage
+
+
+def _verdict(text):
+    m = re.search(r"SUPERVISOR-VERDICT:\s*(ON-COURSE|VIOLATION)\s*(?:\|\s*(.*))?", text or "", re.I)
+    if not m:
+        return None, "no verdict line in the sub-run's reply"
+    if m.group(1).upper() == "ON-COURSE":
+        return "ON-COURSE", None
+    return "VIOLATION", (m.group(2) or "unspecified").strip()
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# ACT on the verdict
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def write_halt(reason):
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(E1DESK, exist_ok=True)
+        with open(HALT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            f.write("%s\n%s\n" % (_now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), reason))
+        return True
+    except Exception:
+        return False
+
+
+def _append_journal(line):
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(BJ.JOURNAL_LOG), exist_ok=True)
+        with open(BJ.JOURNAL_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            f.write(BJ.scrub(line) + "\n")
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def _log(line):
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LOG), exist_ok=True)
+        with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            f.write(BJ.scrub("%s %s" % (_now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), line)) + "\n")
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def run_once(invoke_fn=None, notifier=None):
+    """One supervisor pass. Returns (verdict, reason). Does not build or commit."""
+    g = gather()
+    text, usage = (invoke_fn or _invoke_claude)(_prompt(g))
+    verdict, reason = _verdict(text)
+    st = _state()
+    st["last_run_ts"] = _now().timestamp()
+    st["last_commit"] = g["head"] or st.get("last_commit")
+    st["transcript_events"] = g["transcript_events"]
+    _save_state(st)
+    if usage:
+        try:
+            with open(TOKENS, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                f.write("%s in=%s out=%s cost=%s\n" % (_now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
+                        usage.get("input"), usage.get("output"), usage.get("cost_usd")))
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    if verdict == "VIOLATION":
+        write_halt(reason)
+        line = "SUPERVISOR: halted — %s" % reason
+        _log(line)
+        try:
+            (notifier or _notify)(line)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    else:
+        stamp = "checked %s — on course" % _now().strftime("%H:%M")
+        _append_journal(stamp)
+        _log(stamp if verdict else "check inconclusive (%s) — treated as on course, not halting" % reason)
+    _publish(text, verdict, reason)
+    return verdict, reason
+
+
+def _notify(msg):
+    from scripts import notify
+    notify.notify(msg)
+
+
+def _publish(text, verdict, reason):
+    """The supervisor's own page + scrubbed transcript at site/build/supervisor/."""
+    try:
+        os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
+        tail = []
+        try:
+            tail = open(LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines()[-120:]
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        state = ("HALTED — %s" % reason) if verdict == "VIOLATION" else "on course"
+        body = ("<h1>Supervisor</h1><p class='sub'>Independent watcher, fresh run every 10 minutes in build "
+                "hours. <a href='../index.html'>build room</a></p><p>Now: <b>%s</b></p><pre>%s</pre>"
+                "<h3>Last check (scrubbed)</h3><pre>%s</pre>"
+                % (BJ.scrub(state), BJ.scrub("\n".join(tail)), BJ.scrub((text or "")[-20000:])))
+        with open(os.path.join(OUT, "index.html"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            f.write(BJ._page("Supervisor", body))
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# SILENCE ALARM (called by token_ping)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def silence_line(now=None):
+    """A one-line alarm if no 'checked …' line has appeared for SILENCE_MIN during build hours, else None.
+    Said once per silent stretch (keyed on the stretch start)."""
+    now = now or _now()
+    if not build_hours(now):
+        return None
+    last = _last_checked_ts()
+    if last is None:
+        return None
+    if (now.timestamp() - last) / 60.0 < SILENCE_MIN:
+        return None
+    hhmm = datetime.fromtimestamp(last, IST).strftime("%H:%M")
+    return "SUPERVISOR: silent since %s" % hhmm
+
+
+def _last_checked_ts():
+    """Timestamp of the last 'checked HH:MM — on course' line in the journal, today, or None."""
+    try:
+        lines = [l for l in open(BJ.JOURNAL_LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines() if "checked " in l and "on course" in l]
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+    if not lines:
+        return None
+    m = re.search(r"checked (\d{2}):(\d{2})", lines[-1])
+    if not m:
+        return None
+    n = _now()
+    dt = n.replace(hour=int(m.group(1)), minute=int(m.group(2)), second=0, microsecond=0)
+    if dt > n:
+        dt -= timedelta(days=1)                       # a checked time in the "future" is from before midnight
+    return dt.timestamp()
+
+
+def main():
+    import time
+    while True:
+        try:
+            if build_hours() and due():
+                run_once()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        time.sleep(60)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--once" in sys.argv:
+        print(run_once())
+    else:
+        main()

+_SILENCE_MARK = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".supervisor_silence_posted")
+
+
+def _supervisor_silence_tick():
+    """If the supervisor has gone silent for 30 min during build hours, post ONE line — once per stretch."""
+    try:
+        from scripts import supervisor
+        line = supervisor.silence_line()
+        if not line:
+            return
+        try:
+            last = open(_SILENCE_MARK, encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
+        except Exception:
+            last = ""
+        if line != last:
+            notify(line)
+            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_SILENCE_MARK), exist_ok=True)
+            open(_SILENCE_MARK, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(line)
+    except Exception:
+        pass

+# R-RUNNER-DOWN (owner emergency ruling, 2026-09-22, paper only)
+
+_Archived verbatim per §7.1. A RECORD, never an instruction._
+
+(1) relaunch the book20 runner now, one copy, and confirm on the status page that it polls;
+(2) find why it died and why the watchdog did not relaunch it — if a stale lock file blocked
+    the relaunch, make every lock pid-aware (a lock whose pid is dead is ignored and replaced),
+    test it, and confirm the watchdog relaunches a killed runner within one cycle;
+(3) then the restart (R-RESTART-TONIGHT) after 15:30, then the four missing v2 tabs —
+    Live desk positions, Portfolio, Backtest, Messages — then the rest of the bundle.
+Report each with what the status page shows.

+def _last_runner_poll():
+    """The newer of: the BOOK20 readout mtime, and the shared heartbeat mtime when the runner wrote it last
+    (state == 'book20'). The heartbeat ticks every poll; the readout does not always, so the heartbeat is the
+    truer 'last poll' signal."""
+    try:
+        from engine1_rebuild import scan
+        hb = scan.HEARTBEAT
+    except Exception:
+        hb = None
+    best = None
+    for p, gate_state in ((os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BOOK20-READOUT.json"), None), (hb, "book20")):
+        if not p:
+            continue
+        try:
+            if gate_state:
+                if json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8")).get("state") != gate_state:
+                    continue
+            t = datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(p), IST)
+            best = t if best is None or t > best else best
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return best.isoformat() if best else None
+
+
