+# The five live patterns — in plain words
+
+_The five patterns E1/E10/E11 trade live, after real costs. Paper only — not advice._
+
+## BSE
+- **The rule:** BSE, held for weeks: when how choppy its recent daily returns have been sits in a particular band, its price tends to move in its measured direction.
+- **What it reads:** how choppy its recent daily returns have been
+- **How it's managed:** hold up to 40 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target
+- **Past cases:** 9 trades · **won 100.0%** · **average 0.4937 R after costs**
+- **Fired today:** no
+
+## UNITDSPR
+- **The rule:** UNITDSPR, held for weeks: when its price against a simple moving average sits in a particular band, its price tends to move in its measured direction.
+- **What it reads:** its price against a simple moving average
+- **How it's managed:** hold up to 60 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target
+- **Past cases:** 9 trades · **won 100.0%** · **average 0.4753 R after costs**
+- **Fired today:** no
+
+## ETERNAL
+- **The rule:** ETERNAL, held for weeks: when a momentum reading (RSI) sits in a particular band, its price tends to go UP.
+- **What it reads:** a momentum reading (RSI)
+- **How it's managed:** hold up to 60 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target
+- **Past cases:** 9 trades · **won 100.0%** · **average 0.2069 R after costs**
+- **Fired today:** yes
+
+## AUBANK
+- **The rule:** AUBANK, held for weeks: when its price against the day's volume-weighted average price (VWAP) sits in a particular band, its price tends to go UP.
+- **What it reads:** its price against the day's volume-weighted average price (VWAP)
+- **How it's managed:** hold up to 40 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target
+- **Past cases:** 13 trades · **won 92.3%** · **average 0.1683 R after costs**
+- **Fired today:** yes
+
+## UNITDSPR
+- **The rule:** UNITDSPR, held for weeks: when its price against a moving-average trend line sits in a particular band, its price tends to move in its measured direction.
+- **What it reads:** its price against a moving-average trend line
+- **How it's managed:** hold up to 60 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target
+- **Past cases:** 10 trades · **won 100.0%** · **average 0.061 R after costs**
+- **Fired today:** no

+# The seven YouTube seed rules — plain words + diary status
+
+## Earnings drift (PEAD)  _(Titans Of Tomorrow)_
+- **Rule:** Buy an F&O stock the day after a positive earnings surprise; hold ~40 days.
+- **Needs:** an earnings-surprise feed
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (an earnings-surprise feed not on the desk yet)
+
+## Opening-range breakout  _(Chart Fanatics)_
+- **Rule:** Buy when price breaks above the first 15-minute range; exit at the close or a stop below the range.
+- **Needs:** intraday minute bars
+- **Diary status:** live diary (scoring can start)
+
+## Congressional-trade copying  _(Words of Rizdom)_
+- **Rule:** Buy a US stock within a week of a US lawmaker disclosing a purchase; hold ~3 months.
+- **Needs:** US stock prices + US disclosure filings
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (US stock prices + US disclosure filings not on the desk yet)
+
+## Option variance-risk-premium selling  _(Fabervaale ENG)_
+- **Rule:** Sell a defined-risk index-option spread when implied vol is above realised; close at expiry or a 2x-credit stop.
+- **Needs:** a live option chain
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a live option chain not on the desk yet)
+
+## News continuation  _(Run It Up 858)_
+- **Rule:** Buy a strong positive news move backed by heavy volume; hold a few days on a trailing stop.
+- **Needs:** a news feed
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a news feed not on the desk yet)
+
+## News mean reversion  _(Run It Up 858)_
+- **Rule:** Fade an extreme one-day move (buy after a sharp drop); cover as it reverts over a few days.
+- **Needs:** a news feed (for the trigger)
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a news feed (for the trigger) not on the desk yet)
+
+## Numerology date rules  _(Gary Grinberg (GG33))_
+- **Rule:** Go long/short the index on numerology-significant calendar dates; close on the next such date.
+- **Needs:** only the calendar (no market data)
+- **Diary status:** live diary (scoring can start)

+def _survivors_section():
+    """R-NOW-22 item 1: the five LIVE patterns (survived real execution costs) at the top of the Patterns page,
+    from reports/SURVIVORS.json — the rule, reading, management, record and today's fire, in plain words."""
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        pats = d.get("patterns") or []
+    except Exception:
+        pats = []
+    if not pats:
+        return ""
+    cards = ""
+    for p in pats:
+        cards += ("<div class='panel' style='margin:10px 0'>"
+                  "<div style='display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline'><b style='font-size:15px'>%s</b>"
+                  "%s</div><p style='margin:6px 0'>%s</p>"
+                  "<p class='sub' style='margin:0'>Reads: %s · Managed: %s</p>"
+                  "<p class='sub' style='margin:4px 0 0'>Past cases: %s trades · won %s · average %s after costs</p></div>" % (
+                      esc(p.get("stock")),
+                      ("<span class='pill gain'>fired today</span>" if p.get("fired_today") else "<span class='pill'>no fire today</span>"),
+                      esc(p.get("rule")), esc(p.get("reading")), esc(p.get("management")),
+                      esc(p.get("past_cases")), (pct(p.get("win_pct")) if _present(p.get("win_pct")) else "—"),
+                      (R(p.get("avg_move_after_costs_R")) if _present(p.get("avg_move_after_costs_R")) else "—")))
+    return ("<h2>The five live patterns</h2><p class='sub'>These five cleared real execution costs, so E1/E10/E11 "
+            "trade them. The rest below are on paper diaries. Paper only — not advice; small samples, high bar.</p>"
+            + cards)
+
+

diff --git a/scripts/survivors_readout.py b/scripts/survivors_readout.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..ade98062e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/survivors_readout.py
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+"""R-NOW-22 item 1 — the FIVE LIVE PATTERNS in plain trader words. Reads the live set (the survivors + their
+management), the exam staging (past cases, win rate, average move after costs), and today's fires from the live
+signal path, and writes reports/SURVIVORS.json + .md. The Patterns page renders the same list, and the runner
+posts it once as a 📊. Nothing here trades or changes the live set."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+OUT_JSON = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.json")
+OUT_MD = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.md")
+
+# each reading, in plain words
+READING = {"ema": "its price against a moving-average trend line",
+           "sma": "its price against a simple moving average",
+           "rsi": "a momentum reading (RSI)",
+           "vwap": "its price against the day's volume-weighted average price (VWAP)",
+           "stddev_returns": "how choppy its recent daily returns have been"}
+
+
+def _staging_map():
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        return {"%s|%s" % (str(p["stock"]).upper(), str(p["family"]).lower()): p
+                for p in d.get("certified_patterns", [])}
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _today_fires():
+    """The survivor (symbol, family) -> direction that fired today, from the live signal path. Slow (~a scan);
+    fail-safe to {}."""
+    try:
+        from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner as BR
+        out = {}
+        for c in BR._candidates():
+            out["%s|%s" % (str(BR._sym(c["stock"])).upper(), str(c.get("metric", "")).lower())] = c.get("direction")
+        return out
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def build(write=True, today_fires=None):
+    try:
+        live = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        patterns = live.get("patterns", {})
+    except Exception:
+        patterns = {}
+    stg = _staging_map()
+    fires = today_fires if today_fires is not None else _today_fires()
+    rows = []
+    for key, v in patterns.items():
+        fam = str(v.get("family", "")).lower()
+        stock = str(v.get("stock", "")).upper()
+        s = stg.get(key, {})
+        direction = fires.get(key)
+        dir_words = ("go UP" if str(direction).lower() in ("direct", "long", "buy") else
+                     "go DOWN" if direction else "move in its measured direction")
+        rule = ("%s, held for weeks: when %s sits in a particular band, its price tends to %s." % (
+            stock, READING.get(fam, "a price reading"), dir_words))
+        rows.append({
+            "stock": stock, "reading": READING.get(fam, fam), "rule": rule,
+            "management": v.get("management"),
+            "past_cases": s.get("fires"), "win_pct": s.get("win_pct"),
+            "avg_move_after_costs_R": s.get("mean_net_R"),
+            "fired_today": key in fires,
+        })
+    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r.get("avg_move_after_costs_R") or 0), reverse=True)
+    out = {"note": "The five patterns E1/E10/E11 trade live, after real costs. Paper only — not advice.",
+           "count": len(rows), "patterns": rows, "fired_today": [r["stock"] for r in rows if r["fired_today"]]}
+    if write:
+        with open(OUT_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            json.dump(out, f, indent=1, default=str)
+        _write_md(out)
+    return out
+
+
+def _write_md(o):
+    L = ["# The five live patterns — in plain words", "", "_" + o["note"] + "_", ""]
+    for r in o["patterns"]:
+        L += ["## %s" % r["stock"],
+              "- **The rule:** %s" % r["rule"],
+              "- **What it reads:** %s" % r["reading"],
+              "- **How it's managed:** %s" % (r["management"] or "—"),
+              "- **Past cases:** %s trades · **won %s%%** · **average %s R after costs**" % (
+                  r["past_cases"], r["win_pct"], r["avg_move_after_costs_R"]),
+              "- **Fired today:** %s" % ("yes" if r["fired_today"] else "no"), ""]
+    with open(OUT_MD, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write("\n".join(L))
+
+
+def telegram_text(o=None):
+    o = o or build(write=False)
+    lines = ["\U0001F4CA The five live patterns E1 trades (paper, after real costs):"]
+    for r in o["patterns"]:
+        lines.append("• %s — %s | %s | %s trades, won %s%%, avg %s R after costs%s" % (
+            r["stock"], r["rule"].split(": ", 1)[-1].rstrip("."), r["management"],
+            r["past_cases"], r["win_pct"], r["avg_move_after_costs_R"],
+            " · FIRED TODAY" if r["fired_today"] else ""))
+    fired = o.get("fired_today") or []
+    lines.append("Today: %s" % (", ".join(fired) + " fired" if fired else "no live-set fires yet"))
+    return "\n".join(lines)
+
+
+def _demo():
+    o = build(write=False, today_fires={})
+    assert o["count"] >= 1 and all("rule" in r and "management" in r for r in o["patterns"])
+    t = telegram_text(o)
+    assert t.startswith("\U0001F4CA") and "after costs" in t
+    print("survivors_readout demo OK: %d patterns, plain rule+management+record, 📊 text built." % o["count"])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    else:
+        o = build()
+        print(telegram_text(o))

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..59dfe452b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/youtube_seeds.py
@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+"""R-NOW-22 item 3 (seed rules) — the seven YouTube seed strategies in plain words, each opened as a live
+paper diary (walk-forward is the test). A seed whose data the desk does not have (US stocks, a news feed, an
+option chain that is not recording) is diary-only and honestly BLOCKED-ON-DATA; no fill is fabricated."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+
+# plain-words rule + what data it needs + whether the desk has that data today.
+STATUS = {
+    "Earnings drift (PEAD)": ("Buy an F&O stock the day after a positive earnings surprise; hold ~40 days.",
+                              "an earnings-surprise feed", False),
+    "Opening-range breakout": ("Buy when price breaks above the first 15-minute range; exit at the close or a "
+                               "stop below the range.", "intraday minute bars", True),
+    "Congressional-trade copying": ("Buy a US stock within a week of a US lawmaker disclosing a purchase; hold "
+                                    "~3 months.", "US stock prices + US disclosure filings", False),
+    "Option variance-risk-premium selling": ("Sell a defined-risk index-option spread when implied vol is above "
+                                             "realised; close at expiry or a 2x-credit stop.", "a live option chain", False),
+    "News continuation": ("Buy a strong positive news move backed by heavy volume; hold a few days on a trailing "
+                          "stop.", "a news feed", False),
+    "News mean reversion": ("Fade an extreme one-day move (buy after a sharp drop); cover as it reverts over a "
+                            "few days.", "a news feed (for the trigger)", False),
+    "Numerology date rules": ("Go long/short the index on numerology-significant calendar dates; close on the "
+                              "next such date.", "only the calendar (no market data)", True),
+}
+
+
+def seeds():
+    from engine_youtube import seed_strategies as SS
+    return getattr(SS, "SEEDS", None) or getattr(SS, "STRATEGIES", [])
+
+
+def open_diaries():
+    """Open one live diary per seed (stage 'live diary'). Returns the report rows."""
+    from scripts import diaries
+    rows = []
+    for s in seeds():
+        name = s.get("name", "seed")
+        plain, needs, have = STATUS.get(name, (str(s.get("form", {}).get("entry", "")), "market data", False))
+        diaries.open_diary("YouTube-seed", name, plain)
+        rows.append({"name": name, "channel": s.get("channel"), "rule": plain, "needs": needs,
+                     "data_ready": have,
+                     "diary_status": "live diary (scoring can start)" if have else
+                                     "live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (%s not on the desk yet)" % needs})
+    return rows
+
+
+def _write_report(rows):
+    L = ["# The seven YouTube seed rules — plain words + diary status", ""]
+    for r in rows:
+        L += ["## %s  _(%s)_" % (r["name"], r["channel"]),
+              "- **Rule:** %s" % r["rule"],
+              "- **Needs:** %s" % r["needs"],
+              "- **Diary status:** %s" % r["diary_status"], ""]
+    with open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "YOUTUBE-SEEDS.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write("\n".join(L))
+
+
+def build():
+    rows = open_diaries()
+    _write_report(rows)
+    return rows
+
+
+def _demo():
+    rows = build()
+    assert len(rows) == 7
+    assert any(r["data_ready"] for r in rows) and any(not r["data_ready"] for r in rows)
+    print("youtube_seeds demo OK: 7 seed diaries opened; %d ready, %d blocked-on-data." % (
+        sum(r["data_ready"] for r in rows), sum(not r["data_ready"] for r in rows)))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else [print("%-34s %s" % (r["name"], r["diary_status"])) for r in build()]
